  411: export function digitFromKey(key) {
  412:   const s = String(key || "");
  413:   let h = 0;
  414:   for (let i = 0; i < s.length; i += 1) h = (h * 31 + s.charCodeAt(i)) >>> 0;
  415:   return String(h % 10);
  416: }
  417: 
  418: export function dailyDigitForRow(ymd, grupo2, centena3) {
  419:   return digitFromKey(`${String(ymd || "")}#${String(grupo2 || "")}#${String(centena3 || "")}`);
  420: }
